- **reproai-check**: One-shot static pre-diagnose of a replication package. Scans the working directory, applies the author rule set and a venue profile, and writes 4 JSON reports (5 with a certification target). Never runs author code. Use when an author wants to know if their replication package is ready to submit, or what to fix before a reproducibility check. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/check.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-comply**: Check a replication package against a target journal's replication-package rules (AEA, ...), without re-running the full pre-diagnose. Use when the author only wants the venue compliance checklist. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/comply.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-contribute**: Contribute back to reproai — file a structured GitHub issue for a defect reproai missed, a false flag, a fix that worked, or a new journal venue and its rules. reproai never posts; it hands you a pre-filled issue URL you click Submit on. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/contribute.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-debug**: Rehearse a replication package on a COPY the way a certification service will (R and Python run twice under fixed settings and the outputs compared; Stata run once) — confirm the code runs without errors, that the Table/Figure output commands actually produced files, and whether two runs agree. On a runtime error it does NOT auto-fix; it explains the root cause, offers a few fix options, and asks the author to choose (AskUserQuestion), then applies the chosen fix and re-runs. It executes code but never compares coefficients and never issues a reproducibility verdict. Use after /reproai-fix to… ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/debug.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-fix**: Apply reproai's advisory fixes to a COPY of the package by reading the diagnostic report and rewriting the code yourself, then re-checking until the findings clear. You (the host LLM) do the rewriting under a lossless contract; the deterministic engine only decides what is wrong and re-verifies. Never touches the original; never changes what the code computes. Use after /reproai check when the author wants the recommended fixes applied. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/fix.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-map**: ADVISORY overlay of a manuscript's figures and tables on a replication package's output (write) nodes — which exhibits have no producing output, which outputs no exhibit uses, and panel/count mismatches. LaTeX-only, never runs author code, never issues a reproducibility verdict, and is deliberately NOT part of check. Use when an author wants to see whether every paper exhibit maps to a producing script and vice versa. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/map.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-prepare**: The whole preparation in one pass, from a messy replication folder to a prepared copy with every approved repair recorded, a rehearsal run on a copy (with permission), and a handoff for the destination (a journal's check, a repository, or IQSS Certify); for Certify, it then offers to lead the author to the draft dataset's Certify button. Use when an author wants their package made ready, not only diagnosed. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/prepare.md) · ★19)
- **reproai-update**: Show the installed reproai knowledge version and how to update to the latest rule set. Use when the author asks whether their rules are current, or how reproai stays up to date. ([reproai/reproai](https://github.com/reproai/reproai/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/commands/update.md) · ★19)

- **research**: Default entry point for any research request — a hybrid router that classifies the question deterministically and either delegates to a specialist research skill (pulse for trends/sentiment, grants for NIH funding, litreview for academic literature, syllabus for course reading, patent for prior-ar ([ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine](https://github.com/ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/research.md))
- **Research Brief**: Write a stakeholder-aligned research brief ([Dragoon0x/research-sharp](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/research-sharp/blob/HEAD/rs-strategy/commands/research-brief.md))
- **Research Plan**: Design a complete research plan with methods, timeline, and sample ([Dragoon0x/research-sharp](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/research-sharp/blob/HEAD/rs-user/commands/research-plan.md))
- **Research Roadmap**: Create a quarterly research roadmap aligned to business priorities ([Dragoon0x/research-sharp](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/research-sharp/blob/HEAD/rs-strategy/commands/research-roadmap.md))
- **research-3rd-party-sandboxes**: Research sandbox/test environments for 3rd-party integrations. Delegate, document, update inventory, re-verify. ([buihongduc132/zcode-pi-cmd-loader](https://github.com/buihongduc132/zcode-pi-cmd-loader/blob/HEAD/commands/research-3rd-party-sandboxes.md))
- **research-a-bead**: Run the research-a-bead activity recipe on the named research bead. Loads the research-a-bead skill (which defers to bead-lifecycle-shell for phases A/B/C/D and owns the research-specific variable middle: define-question → search-prior-art → fetch-authoritative-docs → synthesize → file-findings). The drawer + KG triples filed at M5 ARE the deliverable — research beads are no-code by default. ([fkberthold/loom](https://github.com/fkberthold/loom/blob/HEAD/commands/research-a-bead.md))
- **research-account**: Deep-researches a target account using Firecrawl and Exa. Returns structured brief with company snapshot, 90-day triggers, stakeholder map, ICP score, and best outreach hook. Saves to accounts/{slug}-brief.md. ([UitbreidenOS/UitKit](https://github.com/UitbreidenOS/UitKit/blob/HEAD/professional-stacks/gtm_engineer_stack/commands/research-account.md) · ★14)
- **research-account**: Research a company or person and get actionable GTM intel ([propane-ai/kits](https://github.com/propane-ai/kits/blob/HEAD/plugins/GTM/commands/research-account.md) · ★5)
- **research-acquire**: Acquire research papers from acquisition queue, download PDFs, validate integrity, and organize artifacts ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/blob/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/research-complete/elaboration/commands/research-acquire.md) · ★230)
- **research-actors**: Discover public research Actors with the installed Apify CLI before choosing a bounded mobile research route. ([khadinakbarlabs/mobile-app-builder-research](https://github.com/khadinakbarlabs/mobile-app-builder-research/blob/HEAD/commands/research-actors.md))
- **research-aesthetics**: A-line MAGIC investigation — art, material production, visual rhetoric ([warrenghaad/WKSP-1-Ingest-Graph-Review](https://github.com/warrenghaad/WKSP-1-Ingest-Graph-Review/blob/HEAD/.agents/tools/euclid-agent-tools/gea-research/commands/research-aesthetics.md) · 2 copies)
- **research-agent**: Deep competitor research and profiling. Use: /research-agent [company name] ([cortexark/AgentDojo](https://github.com/cortexark/AgentDojo/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/research-agent.md))
- **research-agent-ho**: Research agent for external documentation, best practices, and library APIs ([sameer-thakkar/hackin26-next-deimos](https://github.com/sameer-thakkar/hackin26-next-deimos/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/research-agent-ho.md))
- **research-ai-citations**: Generate high-commercial-intent prompts for a topic, cluster them, and create an audit template for tracking which sources AI tools cite. This is the prompt-driven research counterpart to traditional keyword research. ([TheCraigHewitt/seomachine](https://github.com/TheCraigHewitt/seomachine/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/research-ai-citations.md) · ★7.6k · 4 copies)
- **research-analysis**: Runs the preregistered statistical analysis on collected data: effect sizes with confidence intervals, multiple-comparison correction, robustness checks, deviation log. Use when the user asks to 'run the preregistered analysis' or 'compute effect sizes and CIs'. ([Ahmed-G-Gad/apothem](https://github.com/Ahmed-G-Gad/apothem/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/behavior-diff/install_driver/rendered/opencode/commands/research-analysis.md) · 4 copies)
- **research-analyze**: context_compression: { summary_extraction: 'Extract key points from verbose documentation', redundancy_removal: 'Eliminate duplicate information across sources', relevance_filtering: 'Focus on information relevant to specific goals' } }, ([DNYoussef/Spek-template](https://github.com/DNYoussef/Spek-template/blob/HEAD/docs/api-reference/commands/research-analyze.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
